p.iter(f'{{{ns_hp}}}t'))\n        if text_fragment in full_text:\n            matches.append((p, full_text))\n    return matches\n\ndef remove_linesegarray(p_elem):\n    \"\"\"Remove <hp:linesegarray> ', '2. ', '3.
